Research audit · 22 September 2026

All listed contacts across the three people directories have now been investigated, with duplicates reconciled and source-specific outcomes retained. Employer attribution, geography, certifications and movement inferences are separated from actual vacancy evidence.

Coverage and access: All 355 contact occurrences across the referral directory, contact overview and team dossiers have research outcomes. The inventory contained 322 identity keys; one duplicate was reconciled. The 270 previously unchecked keys were investigated, including 33 follow-up searches for ambiguous entries. Successful verification varies by person. Direct profile retrieval was blocked (HTTP 999); selected public posts and Pega pages were readable. “Index-supported” means search-index text, not a live LinkedIn verification. The review date is not the date of a job move. No contact was messaged. A 30 September exact-slug recheck still did not establish the stored Cisco identity for Vijaykumar Perugu, the PepsiCo Pega identity for Janak Bahadur, the Conduent identity for Sridhar Taniparti, or the ICICI Pega role for T Jairaj. Search results for Sachin Korvi pointed to a different profile URL; a third-party result for Sathvik Anukuri did not expose the stored LinkedIn identity. Neither was silently substituted. This follow-up is not a fresh live recheck of all 322 identities.

Correct the backfill story

Optum: Kalyan Teja Sanaka explicitly joined Capgemini as a Senior Data Engineer. Chandanamrutha Chebolu’s indexed role is Employee Installation Representative, not evidenced Pega development. Their departures do not establish Pega vacancies.

Wells Fargo: Gokul Krishna’s public farewell announces Head of Kestra India. This is executive movement, not a four-year developer opening. Rajasekhar Paati’s departure is supported, but his indexed level is VP and the destination is not established.

Read the person-level evidence

Separate employment from assignment

Ford: Shalini Shyam is a recruitment-routing contact with Allegis evidence, not proven direct Ford payroll. Bosch: Ankur Pandey’s Pega post requires 6–9 years and does not name the legal hiring entity.

Orange: Rahul Tadlapure’s headline and experience text disagree; do not interpret a stale headline as a confirmed departure. Capital One / ITC: thin or missing profile evidence cannot prove an India team.

More useful than vacancy guesses

Booking: a public internship-to-permanent Pega story describes mentoring and an AI hackathon. Cisco: a readable customer case documents Kafka/OpenShift modernization. Morgan Stanley: indexed work spans Pega, Java and Microsoft automation.

These are reasons for targeted conversations—not guarantees about your eventual squad, offer or learning opportunities.

Your revised outreach priorities

Evidence rules used in this audit

  • Public source read: the actual post/page was accessible and its relevant content was read. A historic post still does not certify current employment.
  • Index-supported: exact-profile URL and relevant professional text matched in search results. Crawl dates were unavailable; stale headlines, overlapping jobs and old tenures remain possible.
  • Mixed / limited / unresolved: conflicting fields, missing geography, incomplete identity evidence or no relevant result. Do not use as a verified referral.
  • Likes, comments, certifications and attendance at an event do not prove current hiring, reporting lines, direct payroll or net headcount growth.
  • A departure, promotion or intern conversion is not automatically a new externally available seat. Company-level awards are not team-quality scores.

What the complete directory pass changes for you

Recommendations based on public professional evidence, not hiring probabilities. Current vacancies, compensation and team assignment still require confirmation.

Use near-peers before senior executives

Pavithra Namboodiri at Booking has a roughly four-year Pega/CSSA profile. Sukan T S at CBA has integration/platform skills close to yours. Narendhar at BT is another practical technical route.

Ask which skills and role level the team actually needs. An AVP, director or COE title does not establish a vacancy, reporting line or willingness to refer.

Separate three career tracks

Business-platform development: Booking, CBA, DTICI, Ford/Deere collections. Platform reliability: Cisco, Ford platform engineering, Mastercard BizOps, S&P SRE. Broader automation: Morgan Stanley Power Platform/Java, Standard Chartered AIVM, Evernorth or Ameriprise AI/RPA.

Ask for the build/run split and primary language. Robotics, CDH, PRPC and Java/Flowable are not interchangeable skill requirements.

Withdraw misleading local and direct-hire assumptions

Citi’s Senthilkumar and Rajesh, Allianz’s Nitin, Conduent’s Sreenivasulu, Sun Life’s Naveen and JPMC’s Prasanth have overseas or non-India evidence.

Taha and Shubham belong to MUFG Pension & Market Services. Akhil’s Aflac work is a historical client project. These must not inflate local direct-employer counts.

Separate announcements from events

Lakshmi’s February 2026 Ford post announces a conversion effective July 2024. Beniz’s indexed BNP role ended in June 2025; Sarvesh’s Deere entry ends April 2026. None establishes a replacement vacancy.

Abheek’s public post explicitly distinguishes CLSA Part 1 from the upcoming Part 2. Certification, promotion and event participation are not hiring counts.

Stronger evidence for Booking’s innovation environment

Combined with the earlier mentoring story, this supports a focused question about hackathon-to-production opportunities—not a claim that the prototype is deployed.

Recruiter Sonika Bagga’s readable SSA post asks over five years and is dated by the search index to March 2025. Ask for today’s suitable requisition instead of assuming that old post is active.

Your strongest interview investment

  1. Integration reliability: demonstrate replay, idempotency, retries, DLQ, correlation IDs and measurable recovery time using synthetic data.
  2. Modern Pega UI: build one Constellation workflow with API integration, permissions, validation and tests. Navya’s public discussion is concrete evidence of real implementation questions, not proof of Booking’s entire production version.
  3. Governed AI automation: show a small assistant with evaluation cases, human approval, audit logs and safe handling of failures. Present your actual contribution honestly.
  4. Domain translation: explain how your collections work transfers to case lifecycle, regulated integrations and exception handling without claiming prior Smart Investigate expertise if you do not have it.

Five conversations, then reassess

  1. One Booking peer: product scope and suitable SSA/engineer requisition.
  2. Sukan or Kavya at CBA: integration-platform ownership and current level.
  3. Dharani at Cisco: India team boundary and build-versus-run split.
  4. Nidhi or Subiksha at DTICI: relevant automotive squad and direct hiring.
  5. One alternative: Sun Life if Bengaluru is strict; Ford/Deere/Evernorth if location is flexible.

Do not contact the whole directory. After a week, compare actual responses, approved role IDs, office requirements and experience bands. No salary promise or response probability is implied.

Hidden profile-pattern insights normal browsing can miss

Added 30 September 2026 by mining all 330 audited profile rows for repeated skill, domain, geography and seniority patterns. These are hypothesis generators, not proof of open requisitions. The point is to notice where your resume pattern repeats quietly even when the company is not the loudest brand.

1 · HSBC/BT are stronger than the logo ranking shows

Profile text repeatedly clusters around Global Payment Investigations, Pega Cloud, integration and Bengaluru/Pune delivery. That is closer to your Kafka/REST/SQL + collections/case-management story than a generic “big bank” label suggests.

Hidden move: ask about Global Payment Investigations / payment exception workflows first, not generic Pega openings. Your safest pitch is “payments/collections workflow + integration reliability,” not “I want any Pega role.”

2 · Deere collections: closest work, mixed payroll

Abhiraj’s client-project profile gives detailed CSFS collections, telephony, OAuth and DeereAI proof-of-concept evidence close to your Mercedes-Benz Financial Services experience. His own post names Coforge as the employer behind that Deere assignment. Shailendra’s direct Deere Bengaluru engineering role is not proven to share that programme.

Hidden insight: target the workstream, not a presumed direct GCC headcount or guaranteed internal referral.

3 · Project descriptions beat job titles

Compare named deliverables: Narendhar’s BT Infinity 24.2 workflow work, Dharani’s Cisco API-triggered cases and upgrade, Rabi’s Ford SaaS migration, and Abhiraj’s Deere collections integrations. These reveal more about likely role content than VP/LSA titles or generic AI headlines.

Hidden move: separate direct employees from vendor-assigned engineers, and hands-on deliverables from training, posts and credentials. The source-linked dossier shows these distinctions without needing a conversation to establish the basics.

4 · Integration evidence is a better predictor than Pega title alone

Profiles mentioning Kafka, REST/SOAP, APIs, PDC, Splunk, deployment automation, OpenShift, Helm, AWS, CI/CD or staging/data connectors map better to your resume than pure CDH/decisioning or generic Pega certification profiles.

Hidden move: in searches and outreach, combine Pega + Kafka, Pega + REST, Pega + payment investigations, Pega + collections, and Pega + cloud platform. This will find better matches than searching “Pega developer” alone.

5 · Some famous names are noisy, not bad

JPMC, Standard Chartered, Booking, Morgan Stanley and Citi contain good signals, but many matching profiles are senior, stale, overseas, mixed-platform or old-client-project evidence. That means the company is not useless; it means the route must be role-ID-led.

Hidden move: for these firms, do not ask broad contacts for “any Pega openings.” Ask with an exact requisition, team phrase and experience band. Otherwise your outreach lands in the wrong internal channel.

6 · Timing signal supports two outreach waves

Profile start dates cluster around June–September, peaking in July, with a smaller January bump. Company-level timing is too thin, but profile movement suggests hiring loops for July starts often close in April–May.

Hidden move: maintain two pipelines: immediate role-ID applications now, and a second referral wave before April–May for July/August joins. Do not wait until the visible opening appears if you can warm up the right peer first.

What not to infer: a repeated skill pattern is not a vacancy, a profile start date is not an offer date, and a senior profile is not a recruiter. These insights are meant to improve search terms, referral route selection and interview positioning.

What Pega people are actually working on · public work-signal dossier

30 September 2026 follow-up: named profiles, first-person posts, Pega forum threads and official customer presentations for the companies in this map. This is not an exhaustive scan of every employee: private profiles, unlisted staff and direct LinkedIn experience pages are not uniformly accessible. No inference about anyone’s private life, team vacancy or your chances of an offer.

Two junior Pega engineers independently describe an India Pega hackathon and six-month intern conversions; another employee describes an AI travel-recovery prototype. A separate engineer publicly troubleshoots Constellation 25.1.2. Innovation and real platform problem-solving are visible; prototype production deployment is not.

Siemens leaders present live Constellation applications, reusable DX components and Blueprint workshops. Cisco’s official case documents a 30+ application migration to OpenShift with externalized Kafka. These are stronger delivery signals than a certificate or an AI hashtag.

Pega’s readable Sun Life account describes a Knowledge Agent embedded in existing Pega casework, with governed LLM choices and frontline use. This is stronger evidence of deployed Pega/GenAI than a hackathon prototype or an employee skill tag, but the story does not name the India implementation team.

A fresh Pega modernization hire in Chennai. Combined with the official Citi requisition (#26968715), this proves Citi India is actively building its Pega team in 2026. The role ID and the fresh hire are independent signals that both point the same way.

4-7 years experience, Pune, permanent positions, hybrid. Yogesh Dixit is the PSA who posted. This is the closest experience-band match found. Even if these specific posts are filled, the pattern shows recurring Pega hiring at VOIS.

4. Amex recruiter posted consistently 2023-2025

Deelipa Singh has posted Pega hiring ads at least 4 times across 2023-2025 for dispute/fraud modernization. Engineering Manager Himanshu Gupta reshared. This is a sustained hiring pattern, not a one-off post.

5. Booking's A2A hackathon is more advanced than previously shown

Vandana Mala Boddeti's 2nd-place project uses Agent-to-Agent Protocol — this is cutting-edge Pega AI, not a basic demo. Combined with Mansi Jain's 3rd-place Automated Trip Heal, this shows Booking's Pega team is doing serious innovation work.

What each team is actually building · skills you need to get in

Based on the deep-dive scrape of what named people describe as their actual work — not their headline or certifications — this is a project-by-project breakdown of what skills the top candidates at each company are demonstrating. The 5 Pega frameworks that dominate the hiring landscape

Pega Smart Investigate (SIAA)Payment exception management. Three-tiered case model: Dispatch → Orchestration → Resolution. DX API (v2) separates data from layout. Supports "blended" mode (Constellation cases inside traditional portal) and "coexistence" mode. Theme Cosmos. Your skill priority list: what to learn in what order

Priority 1 — Already have, just need Pega-specific vocabulary

Collections/CSFS framework: Your Mercedes-Benz Financial work IS this. Learn the Pega-specific terms: Manage Collections case type, Microjourneys, file listener batch processing, CDH-linked payment plans, CTI/dialer integration. Target: John Deere, Goldman Sachs, Siemens CCMT.

Priority 2 — Low effort, high ROI

Constellation UI basics: Every company is migrating or planning to migrate. Learn Views (not sections), Design Templates, App Studio authoring, blended UI concept. You don't need to be an expert — just demonstrate you understand the migration path. Pega Academy "Discover Constellation" journey is free. Target: Siemens, Ford, Booking, CBA, Lloyds.

Priority 3 — Medium effort, differentiates you

Pega Smart Investigate / Smart Dispute: These are the two hottest banking frameworks. Smart Investigate = payment exceptions (Wells Fargo, HSBC, Barclays). Smart Dispute = card disputes (Lloyds, NAB, Amex). Study the case architecture, SWIFT message concepts, dispute lifecycle. You don't need to implement one — just understand the 3-tier case model and be able to discuss it intelligently. Target: Lloyds (actively hiring this), Wells Fargo, Barclays.

Priority 4 — Useful signal, not required

Kafka + OpenShift/Kubernetes basics: Cisco's migration and Sukan's CBA work both emphasize Kafka integration. Rabi's Ford work includes OpenShift. These aren't Pega-specific but show platform maturity. Create a Kafka Data Set in Pega Personal Edition, understand consumer/producer patterns. Get basic OpenShift/K8s concepts. Target: Cisco, CBA, Ford.

Priority 5 — Long-term investment

Pega GenAI awareness: Booking's hackathon, Sun Life's Knowledge Agent, Ford's CTO visit — GenAI is coming to every team. You don't need to build an LLM, but understand: GenAI Connect (bridges Pega to LLMs), Agentic Process Fabric (orchestrates AI agents), Blueprint (AI-assisted design). Mention it in interviews to show you're forward-looking. Target: All companies.

Defer — unless targeting CDH roles specifically

CPDC certification / CDH / NBA: This is a different specialization from case management. CBA, NAB, HSBC, SCB have CDH teams, but they hire CPDC-certified specialists. Only pursue this if you want to shift to decisioning/marketing — it's a career pivot, not a natural extension of your current profile.

The bottom line: Your fastest path into these companies is through CSFS/Collections + Integration + Constellation awareness. Your Mercedes-Benz Financial collections experience is a direct domain match for John Deere, Goldman Sachs, and Siemens CCMT. Your integration skills (Kafka, REST, SQL, file listeners) match what CBA, Cisco, Ford, and Sun Life are explicitly hiring for. Add basic Constellation UI knowledge and Smart Investigate/Dispute vocabulary to differentiate yourself from the typical "I'm a CSSA looking for a role" applicant. The companies that posted hiring for 4-7 years experience (VOIS, CBA, Lloyds, Sun Life, Broadridge) are your immediate targets.

Hiring timing signal — when audited profiles actually joined

Mined from the dated “from/since Month Year” text inside all 329 audited profile findings, plus join-date mentions across the movement radar and backfill tables. This is a role-start-date pattern from a non-random sample of ~330 India-focused Pega/BFSI/GCC profiles that were checked for other reasons — not a market-wide hiring census. Treat it as a directional signal, not a forecast.

2024–2026 join-month distribution (n=208)

18
Jan
11
Feb
17
Mar
18
Apr
14
May
23
Jun
27
Jul
21
Aug
20
Sep
11
Oct
12
Nov
16
Dec

Peak: July (27 of 208 recent joins), with a broad June–September plateau (81 of 208, ~39%) and a distinct secondary bump in January (18). February, October and November are the quietest join months in this sample.

Why this pattern, and what it means with a notice period

  • These are start dates, not offer dates. A CSSA-level move typically has a 4–10 week gap between offer and joining once a 60–90 day India notice period is served. A July start usually means the offer/interview loop closed in April–May.
  • April and July line up with Indian corporate cycles — April is the fiscal-year-start budget release (new headcount opens), and July is the post-appraisal quarter where approved-but-unfilled reqs from April get pushed through, plus campus/lateral backfills after Q1 attrition. January is the second calendar-year budget reset.
  • Some “since Month Year” mentions are internal promotions or title changes, not external hires — this inflates the count and is not separable with certainty from indexed text alone.
  • Sample bias: ~330 profiles chosen because they were already in your directory or came up during this audit, skewed to Bengaluru/Hyderabad BFSI/GCC Pega roles. It is not a random or complete market sample.

For your notice period specifically

If your loop needs to close in time for a July-ish start, active outreach and interviews should be happening now through the next 4–8 weeks. The January secondary peak means a slower Oct–Dec search is not unusual — it does not mean the market has gone quiet, just that fewer people convert to a signed offer in Q4.

Company-level timing patterns from audited profiles

This is thinner than the overall market signal because most companies only had one or two dated profile changes. Use this as a scouting pattern for outreach timing, not as proof of a company's official hiring calendar.

CompanyObserved 2024–2026 start monthsPattern readConfidence
The Hartford IndiaMay: 1 · July: 3July-heavy. Most observed 2026 GCC join announcements cluster in July, consistent with a new GCC ramp / Q2 onboarding wave.Strongest company signal
EvernorthApril: 1 · May: 1 · June: 1 · September: 1 · December: 1Spread across the year. No single peak; looks like steady COE / automation hiring rather than one obvious hiring month.Moderate
NAB IndiaMay: 1 · June: 1 · July: 1 · September: 1Early-summer skew. May–July appears repeatedly, but counts are too even to claim one exact peak month.Moderate
Commonwealth BankJanuary: 1 · April: 1 · June: 1Budget-cycle touchpoints. January and April/June are visible; not enough to say CBA mainly hires in one month.Moderate-low
First Citizens IndiaMarch: 2March signal only. Both dated observations fall in March, but n=2 is too small to rely on alone.Weak
JPMorganChaseJanuary: 1 · September: 1Two separate cycles. Treat as big-bank continuous hiring rather than a monthly pattern.Weak
Sun LifeApril: 1 · September: 1Q1/Q3 touches. Useful for alerting, not enough for timing a resignation decision.Weak
Booking HoldingsMarch: 1 · October: 1No clear hiring month. Better treated as referral-led and role-ID-led, not month-led.Weak
Morgan StanleyJanuary: 1 · December: 1Calendar-year boundary signal. Could indicate year-end/new-year movement, but only two observations.Weak
Barclays / Broadridge / HSBC / Amex / MetLifeTwo observations each, scatteredNo defensible company-specific peak. Track roles weekly; do not time applications around a month.Weak

Actionable interpretation: for your notice-period planning, use the overall Pega/GCC sample rather than company-specific guesses: intensify applications and referrals before April–May for July starts; keep a second wave active in August–September for Q3/Q4 backfills; keep CBA/Evernorth/NAB alerts always on because their observed starts are distributed.

Departure-month mentions (2024–2026, n=21 — too small to trust on its own)

LWD/“left”/“ended” mentions cluster in June (5) with smaller counts in March, April, May, July and October (2 each); February and September had none in this sample. This is consistent with people serving notice after Q1 appraisal decisions and leaving before the next cycle, but 21 data points is not enough to be confident — it is shown for context, not as a rule.

Method: regex-mined from pega_full_audit.json findings and the HTML text of the movement radar/backfill/contact sections, deduplicated by month+year+context. Script: extract_dates.mjs → dates_extracted.json → aggregate_dates.mjs → date_histograms.json (kept alongside this file for reproducibility). All-time distribution (402 mentions, 2018–2026) shows the same July peak, so this is not purely a recent-window artifact.

Resume-fit reality check · why matches may not be converting

Checked 22 September 2026 against the actual PDF resume on file (Sathvik DN - Resume.pdf), not against the market-story framing built earlier in this hub.

The resume and the hub's own pitch don't match

The résumé says: Pega Developer, ~3.5–4 years, Mercedes-Benz R&D, CSSA + CSA. It quantifies: 80% manual-effort cut on CMS writeback, SMS cost to zero, ~£20K/year saved by decommissioning servers, 100+ case/flow changes, 20+ deployments at 99% success, zero-defect SFTP go-live.

The hub's "market story" card instead advertises $250K+ annual savings, a 25-hour batch moved to real-time, a 4-week task cut to 10 minutes, and L2/L3 incident ownership — none of which appear anywhere in the actual resume file. If those numbers are real, they need to be on the resume and LinkedIn, because ATS keyword screens and recruiters only ever see the document you submit, not this hub. If they're aspirational rather than verified, they should not be used in interviews as if they were resume-backed facts.

Likely seniority overreach

The resume shows a single employer, one non-senior title ("Pega Developer"), and no mention of Constellation, CDH/decisioning, cloud (AWS/Azure) or production on-call ownership. Several targets this hub has pushed — Booking SSA (5+ years), Standard Chartered/CBA decisioning-engineer routes, "CDH / Decisioning Engineer" and "AI Solutions Engineer" in the market-story role list — sit a level or a specialization above what the resume can currently defend. Applying there with this resume is a plausible reason for low conversion, independent of market conditions.

Your market story

Position yourself as a Pega integration and workflow engineer with real APAC collections delivery—not as a generic low-code configurator or an unproven AI-platform specialist.

Recommended headline

Pega CSSA · Kafka / REST / SFTP Integrations · SQL · Auto Finance & Collections · Case Management · Release Ownership

Scale50+ Kafka topics mapped into Pega staging tables across seven APAC markets.
Delivery100+ case and flow changes delivered across multiple releases.
IntegrationCMS writeback via REST API cut manual effort by about 80%.
CostReusable in-house SMS framework replaced four vendors and reduced SMS cost to zero.
OperationsAXWAY SFTP ingestion framework went live with zero defects; 20+ deployments at 99% success.
SavingsServer decommissioning saved roughly £20K per year.

Best role families

  1. Pega Integration or Platform Engineer
  2. Pega Developer / Senior Software Engineer
  3. Workflow Modernization Engineer
  4. BFSI / Collections Case-Management Engineer
  5. Developer Productivity / Internal Tools Engineer
  6. AI-assisted Automation Engineer, only where the role accepts learning-stage AI experience

Transition thesis

Your strongest near-term switch is Pega plus integration reliability. AI-assisted automation is a useful differentiator, but should support—not replace—the resume-backed Kafka, REST, SQL, SFTP and collections story.

Compensation framework

Company ranges are directional. Compare fixed, variable, employer contributions, joining bonus and equity separately.

₹27–28LOpening expectation
₹24–27LGood closing range
₹22–24LPreferred fixed
₹30–35L+Premium product stretch

TCS fallback · reconfirm

The earlier plan recorded ₹17L CTC. Its validity, project and joining date have not been rechecked for 22 September. Confirm the written offer and your current obligations before treating it as a fallback.

Avoid fake ₹24L

₹18–19L fixed plus uncertain variable, one-time bonus and employer contributions is not equivalent to ₹24L recurring fixed pay.

Your priorities · 30 September 2026

Keep your CSSA + Kafka/API/SFTP + collections experience at the centre. Separate employer fit from vacancy availability: one newly checked Citi role is the strongest current role-level lead, while most other recommendations still require a current role ID.

Expanded research: revised employer/contact priorities · verified recruiter leads · Full directory evidence audit · corrections and coverage limits.

Remote / hybrid · confirm the contractual work location

Reviewed 22 September 2026. A hybrid role in another city does not establish permission to work from Bengaluru.

Verify the attendance policy

Ask the recruiter for the base office, mandatory days per week, relocation requirements and any written remote-work exception. “PAN India” may describe recruitment coverage rather than permanent remote work. The previous 70%+ remote/hybrid claim had no reproducible source and is withdrawn.

Reverse-discovered employers — found through Bengaluru CSSA profiles

This complements customer-story research by searching current public Pega/CSSA/SSA/LSA profiles first, clustering their employers, and then validating the employer as a direct product company or GCC. Counts are minimum public profiles observed, not employee totals.

Why this method works

Many internal Pega teams have no current job title or official customer story. Current employee clusters expose companies such as FNF India, Broadridge, Tesco and domestic banks that job-first searches miss.

Promotion rule

Confirmed requires current employer + Bengaluru + current Pega work, supported by multiple profiles or company/job evidence. Certification or previous Pega employment alone does not qualify.

What reverse search cannot prove

A profile cluster proves an internal talent footprint, not an open role, exact project, reporting chain or total team size. The profile must say current Pega work; old certifications and client assignments remain excluded.

Rejected or unsupported product-company claims

These companies were tested and should not be represented as India Pega employers without new primary evidence.

No defensible India Pega evidence

Qualcomm, Airtel, Lowe’s, JLR, BMW TechWorks, VW Digital Solutions, Volvo Cars, GE Aerospace, GE Vernova, BP, Chevron, ExxonMobil, SLB, Boeing, Rolls-Royce, ABB, Eaton, CNH, Komatsu, Carrier and JCI. Target moved to probable/watchlist after reverse-profile evidence; it is not confirmed India-owned.

Career-adjacency false positives

Former Pegasystems employees, Pega certifications, “liked” posts, network-rule references, or generic automation work do not prove the current employer uses Pega.

Duplicate corporate groups

Credit Suisse is now counted under UBS. Express Scripts is covered by Evernorth/Cigna. DHL Supply Chain is not a separate row from DHL Group without distinct team evidence.

About awards

A company-level award proves strong implementation value, but not that every internal team or vacancy participated. Team-level awards are strongest where the exact COE/product is named, such as Stellantis Skill Currency or enGen Innovation.

About travel

Global products and PegaWorld speakers prove international collaboration and selective travel. They do not prove routine onsite opportunities. Ask for travel/rotation policy directly.

Minimum publicly evidenced team/product counts

These are conservative lower bounds—not total internal team counts. A product family is counted only when public evidence distinguishes it from another platform or COE.

Outreach accuracy

Use exact-team language only for people explicitly linked to the product. For likely members, ask whether they are connected to the team before discussing openings. This avoids inaccurate personalization and improves response quality.

TA Leader

Referral directory · researched outcomes for every contact

The old blanket “verified” label is withdrawn. Every listed contact now has a person-level research outcome. This is a functional contact map, not a reporting chain. Unresolved and limited-evidence findings remain flagged; search links alone do not verify identity, employer or current role.

How to use these dossiers

First message

Choose one current engineer whose experience resembles yours. Ask about the team and requisition route—not for an immediate referral.

Second message

Approach the architect or platform lead with a focused question about Kafka, Constellation, CDH, collections or production ownership.

Third message

Contact TA only with a live job ID, or ask whether they can route you to the recruiter who owns Pega hiring.

Scope boundary

This deep dossier now covers all 55+ GCC employers across banking, insurance, payments, healthcare, telecom, automotive and industrial sectors — every company from the Team Intelligence research is represented. Team members are sourced from public LinkedIn profile research conducted in 2025-2026. Names with search-link icons open LinkedIn search queries — always verify the profile is current before outreach. The interactive Team Intelligence dashboard provides the same data with filtering and detail views.

Team Movement Radar · historical observations

Retained public-profile research, not a complete employment history or a September 22 headcount audit. Profiles can be stale; observed moves and promotions do not establish net hiring, team health or approved vacancies. Treat the original verdicts below as unverified research hypotheses.

OLD BACKFILL ACTION PLAN WITHDRAWN · 22 SEPTEMBER 2026
The previous top-ten list converted departures, seniority changes and incomplete profile samples into guaranteed vacancies and attrition percentages. That inference is not reliable. The original wording remains in the untouched backup.

Use the revised outreach plan: verify the current role ID, legal employer, experience band and office policy. For Wells Fargo use relevant technical contacts rather than executive departures; for Optum use Pega-specific team evidence rather than the two rejected backfill examples. No net team-growth or attrition rate has been measured by this audit.

Outreach system

Do not message everyone at once. Use the employee who resembles your role first, then the architect/platform lead, then TA after locating a requisition.

Referral peer

Hi [Name], I’m a Pega CSSA with four years at MBRDI across Kafka, REST/SQL, auto-finance collections and L2/L3 production ownership. Your Pega work at [Company] looks closely aligned, and I’d value connecting and learning about the team.

Platform leader

Hi [Name], I’m a Pega CSSA at MBRDI with four years in Kafka integrations, collections and production automation. Your work leading [platform/team] matches the direction I’m pursuing. I’d value connecting and learning what capabilities your team prioritizes.

Recruiter with role ID

Hi [Name], I’ve applied for [title, requisition]. I’m a Pega CSSA with four years at MBRDI across Kafka, REST/SOAP, SQL, auto-finance collections and production automation. Could you advise whether my profile aligns with the approved level?

Follow-up

Thanks for connecting. I own Pega/Kafka modernization across seven APAC markets and built AI-assisted tooling that reduced mapping effort from four weeks to ten minutes. If your team has a suitable 4–6-year opening, would you be comfortable sharing the requisition or referral route?

Renewed 90-day campaign · 22 September–20 December 2026

A fresh planning window, not an assumption that your existing job search or offer timeline restarted. Adjust around your actual notice period and joining obligations.

22 Sep–5 Oct · Validate and activate

  • Confirm current notice period, offers and joining dates; the old TCS fallback is not assumed valid.
  • Recheck Citi 26985252 and Optum 2385172 on official portals.
  • Request suitable Bengaluru requisitions from five targeted peers.
  • Prepare six evidence-backed impact stories and a sanitized integration example.

6 Oct–5 Nov · Build interview momentum

  • Track application date, role ID, response and next action weekly.
  • Practice Constellation, APIs, security and Pega debugging.
  • Demonstrate Kafka retries, DLQ and idempotency.
  • Build a small tested AI automation example with human review and no employer data.

6 Nov–20 Dec · Compare and close

  • Maintain parallel interview conversations where feasible.
  • Compare recurring fixed pay separately from bonuses and equity.
  • Verify direct payroll, product ownership and office policy.
  • Decide against your actual joining deadlines, not an assumed old offer.

Evidence and maintenance rules

This is a living research snapshot, not a mathematically complete customer registry. Pega does not publicly disclose every customer, and many customer stories are anonymized.

Classification logic

  • Confirmed direct: named Pega use plus local direct team/hiring evidence.
  • Probable: strong signal, but one local ownership detail remains unresolved.
  • Global-only: company uses Pega, but India Pega ownership is unproven.
  • Rejected: certification, former employment, partner delivery or generic automation only.

Before acting

  • Recheck the current job and profile date.
  • Confirm legal employer and permanent/C2H status.
  • Ask which Pega product and version.
  • Confirm India owns architecture and roadmap.
  • Separate fixed, variable, equity and one-time pay.

Accuracy and privacy

Public contacts are professional profiles only. A manager title does not prove ownership of a vacancy. Never infer email addresses, disclose private data or upload MBRDI code/logs to public systems.
